Observed telemetry

Trigger ranges

The mode (housing) value is a fresh uniform random draw inside the range each time a tier fires. You cannot know the exact trigger by watching — but the meter is the live value, and the closer it is to the top of its range, the higher your chance that your very next eligible play is the one that crosses it.

tap a column to inspect the win at that index
loss <5× <20× <200× 200×+
RULE // 01

What you enter (exactly)

1. Game — the title on the screen (5 choices).
2. Bet amount — the denomination you are playing. Must stay the same for the whole session.
3. One entry per spin, in order, no gaps:

• A win → type the cash amount shown (e.g. 2.50), then ADD.
• A loss → tap LOSS (0.00).
• Amounts are the ones on the win meter, in dollars and cents.

Free / Win-A-Spin rounds: paid at $0.25 and advance the $0.25 index. If you see one, switch Bet to $0.25 and Reset — don't mix it into a higher-bet session (the app warns you if an amount can't fit).

Bonus rounds (Kong Spins, respins, etc.): only the final total paid matters — enter that total as one win. Board-by-board counts are cosmetic.

No advance / round did not complete: don't enter anything.

Undo removes the last entry; Reset starts the session over.

This only profits when the index advances for free (other players on a shared pool, or free spins). Paying to advance it yourself returns ~0.86× on average.

RULE // 02

Jackpot tab inputs

Type the current meter value (the dollars shown on the top-box jackpot display) for each tier, and leave it — the app compares it to that tier's trigger range and your current bet.

Grand only qualifies at a bet ≥ $1.00. The exact firing value is a hidden random draw inside the range; the higher the meter, the higher your chance the very next eligible spin is the one.

RULE // 03

How prediction works

Each denomination has its own 60,000-entry pool; every spin advances the index by one and pays multiplier × bet = pool[index] × bet. Feeding results narrows the possible index to one, then the app reads the pool ahead and tells you the next win, the next loss, and the best forward run.

One bet per session and no gaps are the two rules that make it lock.

Once locked, the plan card shows the exact most-profitable forward run (net, spins, the seat it stops at) plus the next-best alternatives ranked by net, with BEST ROI and FASTEST tags. Red net = no profitable exit nearby — don't play.

Target win $ — optional. Enter a minimum win (e.g. 100). The app then ignores every win smaller than that and reports the total cost (spins × bet) to reach the first win ≥ the target, plus how many spins you must pay through to get there (you cannot skip). Set it to 0.00 to turn it off.
